# Text IBM's engine cannot survive.
#
# Every string here takes an unhandled page fault in the original, and ours
# took the same one until the guards in src/delta.c went in. There is nothing
# to compare the audio against, since the reference produces none for any of
# them, so test/crashers.sh checks only that ours answers: either it speaks
# the word or it gives the utterance up, and the process is still there.
#
# These were found rather than listed. tools/crash-search.py starts from text
# that kills the engine, tries every one-letter change to it, keeps whatever
# kills it too, and does the same to those. Three seeds -- the word this
# started from and two misspellings of Wednesday -- became 230 after one round
# and 10,751 after two.
#
# A corpus grown that way covers the shapes it was grown from and nothing
# else, and taking each guard out on its own is what says so. Those three
# seeds are letters, so a sweep of 145,295 strings carrying apostrophes and
# clock times was wanted for the guards such text reaches: 142 more, and
# 20,399 after a round. One guard was still moving no case after that, so its
# own family was seeded and grown as well, which is the last 127.
#
# Do not read this file aloud through a synthesiser that has not been fixed.
